    44	
    45	/** Split the note text into a Wealthbox-ready (title, body) pair: title is
    46	 *  the first non-blank line, body is everything after it (or the same text
    47	 *  again when the note is a single line, so the body is never empty). */
    48	function splitNoteForCrm(text: string): { title: string; body: string } {
    49	  const trimmed = text.trim();
    50	  const newlineIdx = trimmed.indexOf('\n');
    51	  if (newlineIdx === -1) return { title: trimmed, body: trimmed };
    52	  return { title: trimmed.slice(0, newlineIdx).trim(), body: trimmed.slice(newlineIdx + 1).trim() };
    53	}
    54	
    55	/** How often (ms) we debounce-write the disk mirror. */
    56	const DISK_MIRROR_DEBOUNCE_MS = 2000;
    57	
    58	interface MatterNotesEditorProps {
    59	  matter: Matter;
    60	  /** The running sync client. Null triggers the fail-closed render. */
    61	  syncClient: MatterSyncClient | null;
    62	  /**
    63	   * WorkspaceService instance used to mirror notes to disk. If omitted the
    64	   * disk mirror is skipped (e.g. in tests without a workspace).
    65	   */
    66	  workspaceService?: { writeFile: (path: string, content: string) => Promise<void> } | null;
    67	  className?: string;
    68	}
    69	
    70	/** Label the sync status badge for the user (i18n key suffix). */
    71	function statusI18nKey(
    72	  status: ReturnType<typeof useMatterSyncStatus>,
    73	): string {
    74	  switch (status) {
    75	    case 'live': return 'status-live';
    76	    case 'connecting': return 'status-connecting';
    77	    case 'catching-up': return 'status-catching-up';
    78	    case 'offline': return 'status-offline';
    79	    case 'error': return 'status-error';
    80	    default: return 'status-idle';
    81	  }
    82	}
    83	
    84	function statusDotClass(
    85	  status: ReturnType<typeof useMatterSyncStatus>,
    86	): string {
    87	  switch (status) {
    88	    case 'live': return 'bg-green-500';
    89	    case 'connecting':
    90	    case 'catching-up': return 'bg-yellow-400';
    91	    case 'offline': return 'bg-gray-400';
    92	    case 'error': return 'bg-red-500';
    93	    default: return 'bg-gray-300';
    94	  }
    95	}
    96	
    97	export function MatterNotesEditor({
    98	  matter,
    99	  syncClient,
   100	  workspaceService,
   101	  className,
   102	}: MatterNotesEditorProps) {
   103	  const { t } = useTranslation();
   104	  const syncStatus = useMatterSyncStatus(matter.id);
   105	  const editorContainerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
   106	  const editorViewRef = useRef<EditorView | null>(null);
   107	  const diskMirrorTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
   108	  const [noteText, setNoteText] = useState(() => {
   109	    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-base-to-string -- Y.Text.toString() is the documented way to read its content (same pattern as the EditorState.create() call below).
   110	    return syncClient?.doc.getText('notes').toString() ?? '';
   111	  });
   112	  // Codex review catch (P2): if this component instance is reused for a
   113	  // DIFFERENT client (syncClient changes without unmounting), the lazy
   114	  // useState initializer above only ran once — noteText would keep the
   115	  // previous client's text until the user typed, and "Send to Wealthbox"
   116	  // could queue the wrong client's note under the new matter.id. React's
   117	  // "adjust state during render" pattern resyncs it without the cascading-
   118	  // render effect-setState lint issue.
   119	  const [prevSyncClient, setPrevSyncClient] = useState(syncClient);
   120	  if (syncClient !== prevSyncClient) {
   121	    setPrevSyncClient(syncClient);
   122	    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-base-to-string -- see above.
   123	    setNoteText(syncClient?.doc.getText('notes').toString() ?? '');
   124	  }
   125	  const [sentConfirmation, setSentConfirmation] = useState(false);
   126	  const enqueueCrmWrite = useCrmWriteQueueStore((s) => s.enqueue);
   127	  // BUG-032 (data loss): flushes the latest pending disk-mirror write. Set while
   128	  // a write is debounced; called on unmount so navigating away within the
   129	  // debounce window doesn't silently drop the last lines of notes. Cleared once
   130	  // the timer fires normally so a later unmount never double-writes.
   131	  const flushDiskMirrorRef = useRef<(() => void) | null>(null);
   132	
   133	  const writeDiskMirror = useCallback(
   134	    async (text: string) => {
   135	      if (!workspaceService) return;
   136	      const folder = matter.folderPaths[0];
   137	      if (!folder) return;
   138	      try {
   139	        await workspaceService.writeFile(`${folder}/matter-notes.md`, text);
   140	      } catch (err) {
   141	        // Non-fatal: the Yjs doc is the source of truth.
   142	        console.warn('[MatterNotesEditor] disk mirror write failed:', err);
   143	      }
   144	    },
   145	    [workspaceService, matter.folderPaths],
   146	  );
   147	
   148	  // Schedule a debounced write of the notes text to the first matter folder.
   149	  const scheduleDiskMirror = useCallback(
   150	    (text: string) => {
   151	      if (!workspaceService) return;
   152	      const folder = matter.folderPaths[0];
   153	      if (!folder) return;
   154	      if (diskMirrorTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(diskMirrorTimerRef.current);
   155	      flushDiskMirrorRef.current = () => { void writeDiskMirror(text); };
   156	      diskMirrorTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
   157	        flushDiskMirrorRef.current = null;
   158	        void writeDiskMirror(text);
   159	      }, DISK_MIRROR_DEBOUNCE_MS);
   160	    },
   161	    [workspaceService, matter.folderPaths, writeDiskMirror],
   162	  );
   163	
   164	  useEffect(() => {
   165	    if (!editorContainerRef.current || !syncClient) return;
   166	    // Already mounted (StrictMode double-effect).
   167	    if (editorViewRef.current) return;
   168	
   169	    const yText = syncClient.doc.getText('notes');
   170	
   171	    // Light theme matching the rest of the markdown editor. We keep it minimal:
   172	    // no line numbers (notes are short), same font size + monospace as MarkdownEditor.
   173	    const lightTheme = EditorView.theme(
   174	      {
   175	        '&': {
   176	          height: '100%',
   177	          fontSize: '14px',
   178	          fontFamily: 'ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, Monaco, Consolas, monospace',
   179	          background: '#ffffff',
   180	        },
   181	        '.cm-content': {
   182	          padding: '12px 16px',
   183	          minHeight: '100%',
   184	          caretColor: '#1a1a1a',
   185	          color: '#1a1a1a',
   186	        },
   187	        '.cm-focused': { outline: 'none' },
   188	        '.cm-editor': { height: '100%' },
   189	        '.cm-scroller': { overflow: 'auto', height: '100%' },
   190	        '.cm-gutters': { background: '#f9fafb', borderRight: '1px solid #e5e7eb', color: '#9ca3af' },
   191	        '.cm-cursor': { borderLeftColor: '#1a1a1a' },
   192	        '.cm-selectionBackground': { background: '#dbeafe !important' },
   193	        '.cm-activeLineGutter': { background: '#f3f4f6' },
   194	      },
   195	      { dark: false },
   196	    );
   197	
   198	    // y-codemirror.next: yCollab with null awareness (no presence channel).
   199	    // Passing `false` for undoManager so UndoManager doesn't interfere with
   200	    // the app's own undo stack on close.
   201	    const collabExtension = yCollab(yText, null, { undoManager: false });
   202	
   203	    // Mirror text to disk on every local doc update (debounced), and keep
   204	    // noteText in sync so the "Send to Wealthbox" button reflects the live
   205	    // content (including remote peers' edits, which yCollab applies as a
   206	    // CodeMirror transaction too).
   207	    const diskMirrorExtension = EditorView.updateListener.of((update) => {
   208	      if (update.docChanged) {
   209	        const text = update.state.doc.toString();
   210	        scheduleDiskMirror(text);
   211	        setNoteText(text);
   212	      }
   213	    });
   214	
   215	    const state = EditorState.create({
   216	      doc: yText.toString(),
   217	      extensions: [
   218	        history(),
   219	        keymap.of([...defaultKeymap, ...historyKeymap]),
   220	        drawSelection(),
   221	        syntaxHighlighting(defaultHighlightStyle),
   222	        markdown({ base: markdownLanguage }),
   223	        lightTheme,
   224	        collabExtension,
   225	        diskMirrorExtension,
   226	      ],
   227	    });
   228	
   229	    const view = new EditorView({
   230	      state,
   231	      parent: editorContainerRef.current,
   232	    });
   233	    editorViewRef.current = view;
   234	
   235	    return () => {
   236	      if (diskMirrorTimerRef.current) {
   237	        clearTimeout(diskMirrorTimerRef.current);
   238	        // Flush the latest notes so leaving within the debounce window doesn't
   239	        // silently drop the last lines (BUG-032, data loss).
   240	        flushDiskMirrorRef.current?.();
   241	      }
   242	      view.destroy();
   243	      editorViewRef.current = null;
   244	    };
   245	  }, [syncClient, scheduleDiskMirror]);
   246	
   247	  const handleSendToWealthbox = useCallback(() => {
   248	    const { title, body } = splitNoteForCrm(noteText);
   249	    if (!title) return;
   250	    enqueueCrmWrite({ kind: 'note', matterId: matter.id, title, body, sourceRef: `note:${matter.id}` });
   251	    setSentConfirmation(true);
   252	    setTimeout(() => { setSentConfirmation(false); }, SEND_CONFIRMATION_MS);
   253	  }, [noteText, matter.id, enqueueCrmWrite]);
   254	
   255	  // Fail-closed: no sync client means no access.
   256	  if (!syncClient) {
   257	    return (
   258	      <div
   259	        data-testid="matter-notes-no-access"
   260	        className={cn('flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full text-center px-6', className)}
   261	      >
   262	        <div className="text-4xl mb-3 opacity-40">&#128274;</div>
   263	        <p className="text-sm font-medium text-foreground/80 mb-1">
   264	          {t('matter.notes.no-access')}
   265	        </p>
   266	        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground max-w-xs">
   267	          {t('matter.notes.no-access-hint')}
   268	        </p>
   269	      </div>
   270	    );
   271	  }
   272	
   273	  return (
   274	    <div
   275	      data-testid="matter-notes-editor"
   276	      className={cn('flex flex-col h-full bg-white', className)}
   277	    >
   278	      {/* Header: matter name + sync status badge + subtitle */}
   279	      <div className="flex items-start justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b bg-gray-50">
   280	        <div className="min-w-0">
   281	          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   282	            <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-900 truncate">
   283	              {t('matter.notes.title')}
   284	            </h2>
   285	            {/* Sync status badge */}
   286	            <span
   287	              data-testid="matter-notes-sync-badge"
   288	              className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-gray-500 shrink-0"
   289	              aria-label={t(`matter.notes.${statusI18nKey(syncStatus)}`)}
   290	            >
   291	              <span className={cn('inline-block w-2 h-2 rounded-full', statusDotClass(syncStatus))} />
   292	              {t(`matter.notes.${statusI18nKey(syncStatus)}`)}
   293	            </span>
   294	          </div>
   295	          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">
   296	            {t('matter.notes.subtitle')}
   297	          </p>
   298	        </div>
   299	        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
   300	          {sentConfirmation && (
   301	            <span data-testid="matter-notes-sent-confirmation" className="text-xs text-emerald-700">
   302	              {t('matter.notes.sent-to-wealthbox')}
   303	            </span>
   304	          )}
   305	          <button
   306	            type="button"
   307	            data-testid="matter-notes-send-to-wealthbox"
   308	            onClick={handleSendToWealthbox}
   309	            disabled={noteText.trim() === ''}
   310	            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-gray-700 border border-gray-300 rounded-md px-2.5 py-1.5 bg-white hover:bg-gray-50 disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-default disabled:hover:bg-white"
   311	          >
   312	            <Send className="w-3.5 h-3.5" strokeWidth={1.75} />
   313	            {t('matter.notes.send-to-wealthbox')}
   314	          </button>
   315	        </div>
   316	      </div>
   317	
   318	      {/* Editor surface */}
   319	      <div
   320	        ref={editorContainerRef}
   321	        data-testid="matter-notes-cm-editor"
   322	        className="flex-1 overflow-hidden"
   323	        aria-label={`${matter.name} shared notes`}
   324	      />
   325	    </div>
   326	  );
   327	}

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	/**
     2	 * openMatterNotes — entry-point contract for opening the shared notes surface.
     3	 *
     4	 * Opens (or focuses) the notes tab for a given local matter. Uses the app's
     5	 * existing tab mechanism: a synthetic path `matter-notes:/<localMatterId>` is
     6	 * opened as an `openTab` call with type `'file'`. MainPanel's render switch
     7	 * detects the `matter-notes:/` prefix and renders MatterNotesEditor instead of
     8	 * the default file content.
     9	 *
    10	 * This function is safe to call from anywhere that has access to the editor
    11	 * store (it reads state without subscribing to React).
    12	 *
    13	 * Contract: `export function openMatterNotes(localMatterId: string): void`
    14	 */
    15	
    16	import { useEditorStore } from '@/platform/state/editorStore';
    17	import { useMatterStore } from '@/platform/matter/matterStore';
    18	import i18n from '@/i18n';
    19	
    20	/**
    21	 * Open or focus the shared notes surface for a matter.
    22	 *
    23	 * If the tab is already open, this focuses it without re-creating it.
    24	 * If the matter does not exist, or is not shared with a firm, this is a
    25	 * no-op. Opening notes for an unshared matter makes no sense: there is no
    26	 * firm matter ID to sync against.
    27	 */
    28	export function openMatterNotes(localMatterId: string): void {
    29	  const matter = useMatterStore.getState().matters.find((m) => m.id === localMatterId);
    30	  if (!matter || !matter.shared || !matter.firmMatterId) return;
    31	
    32	  const tabPath = `matter-notes:/${localMatterId}`;
    33	  const { openTabs, setActiveTab, openTab } = useEditorStore.getState();
    34	
    35	  // If the tab is already open, just focus it.
    36	  const existing = openTabs.find((t) => t.path === tabPath);
    37	  if (existing) {
    38	    setActiveTab(tabPath);
    39	    return;
    40	  }
    41	
    42	  // F-125: use matterLabel (not matter.name) so the tab title always shows the
    43	  // best human-readable label — avoids leaking raw IDs if matter.name is empty
    44	  // or if name === client (both set to client_name on firm-linked matters).
    45	  const label = matter.client.trim() || matter.name.trim() || (matter.firmMatterId ?? matter.id);
    46	  const tabTitle = i18n.t('matter.notes.tab-title', { name: label });
    47	
    48	  // Open a new notes tab.
    49	  openTab(
    50	    tabPath,
    51	    tabTitle,
    52	    '', // Notes content is managed by the Yjs doc, not the tab content field.
    53	    'file',
    54	  );
    55	}

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	/**
     2	 * MatterNotesEditorWrapper — resolves the matter from the store and manages the
     3	 * MatterSyncClient lifecycle for the notes tab.
     4	 *
     5	 * This is the React boundary between the tab system (which knows only the
     6	 * localMatterId string) and the MatterNotesEditor (which needs the resolved
     7	 * Matter object + a running MatterSyncClient). It boots the sync client on
     8	 * mount and stops it when the tab unmounts.
     9	 */
    10	
    11	import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
    12	import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
    13	import { useMatterStore } from '@/platform/matter/matterStore';
    14	import { ensureMatterSync, getMatterSyncClient } from '@/features/matters/logic/matterNotesSync';
    15	import { MatterNotesEditor } from '@/features/matters/MatterNotesEditor';
    16	import { useMatterSyncStatus } from '@/platform/matter/matterSyncStore';
    17	import type { MatterSyncClient } from '@/platform/firm/MatterSyncClient';
    18	
    19	interface MatterNotesEditorWrapperProps {
    20	  localMatterId: string;
    21	  workspaceService?: { writeFile: (path: string, content: string) => Promise<void> } | null;
    22	  className?: string;
    23	}
    24	
    25	export function MatterNotesEditorWrapper({
    26	  localMatterId,
    27	  workspaceService,
    28	  className,
    29	}: MatterNotesEditorWrapperProps) {
    30	  const { t } = useTranslation();
    31	  const matter = useMatterStore((s) => s.matters.find((m) => m.id === localMatterId) ?? null);
    32	  const [syncClient, setSyncClient] = useState<MatterSyncClient | null>(null);
    33	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    34	  // Subscribe to sync status so we detect when stopMatterSync evicts our client
    35	  // (e.g. after a key-epoch advance fails with 403/walled).
    36	  const syncStatus = useMatterSyncStatus(localMatterId);
    37	
    38	  useEffect(() => {
    39	    if (!matter) {
    40	      setLoading(false);
    41	      return;
    42	    }
    43	    let cancelled = false;
    44	    // Only show the loading spinner on the initial mount or when our client has
    45	    // been evicted from the cache (e.g. after a walled key-epoch advance). For
    46	    // normal status transitions (connecting → live) the current syncClient is
    47	    // still valid and we don't want to flash the loading state.
    48	    const cachedClient = getMatterSyncClient(matter.id);
    49	    if (!cachedClient) {
    50	      setLoading(true);
    51	    }
    52	    void ensureMatterSync(matter).then((client) => {
    53	      if (cancelled) return;
    54	      setSyncClient(client);
    55	      setLoading(false);
    56	    });
    57	    return () => {
    58	      cancelled = true;
    59	      // Do NOT stop the client on unmount — the notes tab can be closed and
    60	      // re-opened; the singleton cache in matterNotesSync handles reuse.
    61	      // stopMatterSync is called explicitly on unshare / sign-out.
    62	    };
    63	  }, [matter?.id, syncStatus]); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    64	
    65	  if (loading) {
    66	    return (
    67	      <div className={`flex items-center justify-center h-full text-muted-foreground text-sm ${className ?? ''}`}>
    68	        <span>{t('matter.notes.loading')}</span>
    69	      </div>
    70	    );
    71	  }
    72	
    73	  if (!matter) {
    74	    return (
    75	      <div className={`flex items-center justify-center h-full text-muted-foreground text-sm ${className ?? ''}`}>
    76	        <span>{t('matter.notes.not-found')}</span>
    77	      </div>
    78	    );
    79	  }
    80	
    81	  return (
    82	    <MatterNotesEditor
    83	      matter={matter}
    84	      syncClient={syncClient}
    85	      {...(workspaceService != null ? { workspaceService } : {})}
    86	      {...(className != null ? { className } : {})}
    87	    />
    88	  );
    89	}

     2	//
     3	// This REPLACES the old lossy Mammoth/TipTap editor. It renders the JSON DOM
     4	// produced by the in-house OOXML engine (`docx_open`) faithfully — paragraphs
     5	// with common formatting, runs, tracked insertions (green/underline) and
     6	// deletions (red/strikethrough) with author tooltips, and comment ranges — and
     7	// drives accept/reject of tracked changes through the engine
     8	// (`docx_resolve_revision` / `docx_resolve_all`). Edits and resolutions persist
     9	// to the real `.docx` via `docx_save`, which preserves every unmodeled part of
    10	// the package (styles, numbering, theme, headers/footers, media, tables).
    11	//
    12	// LAYOUT (locked design): a slim top bar (file name · Reviewing toggle · save
    13	// status), a clean document surface (white page on light-gray canvas, generous
    14	// margins, letterhead-friendly), and a right-side Review pane listing changes
    15	// grouped by revision with per-change Accept/Reject + Accept all / Reject all,
    16	// plus a comments display. LIGHT THEME ONLY; navy accent (var(--kp-navy)).
    17	//
    18	// ENGINE COUPLING: the engine reads/writes by FILE PATH, not the data-URL the
    19	// rest of the tab pipeline carries. So this editor takes `filePath` and is the
    20	// source of truth for the `.docx` on disk; it does not push data-URL content
    21	// back up through `onContentChange`. In the browser / test environment (no
    22	// native engine) it degrades to the read-only `DocxViewer` with a notice.
    23	//
    24	// A4 SEAM: `applyResolvedDocument` is the single choke point that swaps the
    25	// in-memory DOM after any engine call and schedules a save. The future AI

    27	// returned DOM through `applyResolvedDocument`. `onDocumentChange` is exposed so

codex
The Word editor has a “Draft follow-up” action mounted, but no Wealthbox action. The only queue source I found is shared notes, and its opener is hidden inside Matter Manager only for already-shared firm clients. That explains why the smoke found nothing in normal document/client surfaces.

     1	// CRM write-back approval queue.
     2	//
     3	// Holds proposed CRM writes (notes/tasks) drafted from a client's note or
     4	// meeting until the advisor explicitly approves them via the review card.
     5	// No persistence: proposals are session-scoped by design — a restart clears
     6	// un-approved proposals, which is the safe default (nothing half-sent
     7	// survives a crash into a new session unexpectedly).
     8	//
     9	// The ONLY call sites of `approve()` are the review card's Approve button
    10	// and this file's own tests. Enqueuing never sends anything.
    11	
    12	import { create } from 'zustand';
    13	
    14	import { crmCreateNote, crmCreateTask, crmUpdateField } from '@/platform/utils/wealthbox-commands';
    15	import { composeFieldBlend, isWritableField } from '@/platform/state/fieldBlend';
    16	import type { Provider } from '@/platform/providers/Provider';
    17	
    18	export type CrmWriteStatus = 'proposed' | 'sending' | 'sent' | 'failed' | 'verify_pending' | 'stale';
    19	
    20	export interface ProposedCrmWrite {
    21	  id: string;
    22	  kind: 'note' | 'task' | 'field';
    23	  matterId: string;
    24	  title: string;
    25	  body: string;
    26	  dueDate?: string;
    27	  sourceRef: string;
    28	  status: CrmWriteStatus;
    29	  remoteId?: string;
    30	  error?: string;
    31	  /**
    32	   * Identifies THIS approval event (an RFC3339 timestamp), not the content —
    33	   * set once the first time this item is sent, then reused verbatim on every
    34	   * retry of that same item. The backend's dedup ledger includes it in the
    35	   * write's idempotency key: a retry with the same requestedAt collides with
    36	   * (and is safely suppressed as) its own earlier attempt, while a later,
    37	   * separate approval of identical content gets a fresh item (and thus a
    38	   * fresh requestedAt) and is never mistaken for a duplicate. See
    39	   * `crm_create_note`'s doc comment in `src-tauri/src/commands/crm/commands.rs`.
    40	   */
    41	  requestedAt?: string;
    42	  /**
    43	   * Task 9c — field-level blended update (`kind: 'field'` only). `field` is
    44	   * the provider field path (e.g. `background_information`); `existingValue`
    45	   * /`newValue` are the 3-column review's reference columns; `finalValue` is
    46	   * the user-editable blend that actually gets written. `existingValue` is
    47	   * REPLACED with the fresh live value if the backend's stale-guard rejects
    48	   * the write because the field drifted since the proposal was drafted (see
    49	   * the 'stale' status below).
    50	   */
    51	  field?: string;
    52	  existingValue?: string;
    53	  newValue?: string;
    54	  finalValue?: string;
    55	}
    56	
    57	interface CrmWriteQueueState {
    58	  items: ProposedCrmWrite[];
    59	  enqueue: (item: Omit<ProposedCrmWrite, 'id' | 'status'>) => void;
    60	  approve: (ids: string[], householdKey: string) => Promise<void>;
    61	  dismiss: (id: string) => void;
    62	  /** Task 9c: the advisor editing a field item's Blended column. `kind:
    63	   *  'field'` items only — a no-op on any other item. */
    64	  updateFinalValue: (id: string, finalValue: string) => void;
    65	  /**
    66	   * Task 9c: the ONLY way to enqueue a field-level blended update — computes
    67	   * `finalValue` via `composeFieldBlend` (scalar replace / narrative merge /
    68	   * deterministic fallback) before the item ever reaches the queue, so a
    69	   * caller can never enqueue a field proposal with a blank blend. Calling
    70	   * `enqueue()` directly with `kind: 'field'` and no `finalValue` is a bug —
    71	   * this is the real entry point for that item shape.
    72	   */
    73	  enqueueFieldUpdate: (
    74	    args: {
    75	      matterId: string;
    76	      title: string;
    77	      field: string;
    78	      existingValue: string;
    79	      newValue: string;
    80	      sourceRef: string;
    81	    } & (
    82	      | { provider?: undefined; onBeforeProviderCall?: undefined }
    83	      // Codex review catch (P2): onBeforeProviderCall is REQUIRED whenever a
    84	      // provider is passed — mirrors composeFieldBlend's own constraint, so
    85	      // the required egress-audit hook can't be silently dropped at this
    86	      // (the real) entry point either. See fieldBlend.ts's doc comment.
    87	      | { provider: Provider; onBeforeProviderCall: (prompt: string) => void }
    88	    ),
    89	  ) => Promise<void>;
    90	}
    91	
    92	function newId(): string {
    93	  return `crmw-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`;
    94	}
    95	
    96	// Codex review catch (P2): a bare `new Date().toISOString()` is not
    97	// guaranteed unique — two DIFFERENT items approved within the same
    98	// millisecond (a real risk: approve() can move on to its next item as soon
    99	// as a fast mocked/local call resolves) would get an identical requestedAt,
   100	// and since the backend's dedup key doesn't include matterId/sourceRef, the
   101	// second legitimate write would be silently treated as a retry of the first
   102	// and dropped. Track the last-issued millisecond and always bump forward by
   103	// at least 1ms, so a fresh requestedAt is strictly monotonic within this
   104	// session no matter how fast two approvals fire back to back.
   105	let lastRequestedAtMs = 0;
   106	function newRequestedAt(): string {
   107	  const now = Date.now();
   108	  lastRequestedAtMs = now > lastRequestedAtMs ? now : lastRequestedAtMs + 1;
   109	  return new Date(lastRequestedAtMs).toISOString();
   110	}
   111	
   112	function setItem(id: string, patch: Partial<ProposedCrmWrite>) {
   113	  useCrmWriteQueueStore.setState((state) => ({
   114	    items: state.items.map((item) => (item.id === id ? { ...item, ...patch } : item)),
   115	  }));
   116	}

   118	/** Like `setItem`, but drops `error` entirely (retry clearing a prior failure). */

   120	  useCrmWriteQueueStore.setState((state) => ({

   130	// "this field changed in the CRM since the proposal — current value: {0}".
   131	// The captured group can legitimately contain newlines (it's the live field
   132	// content), hence the /s flag.
   133	const STALE_FIELD_VALUE_RE = /this field changed in the CRM since the proposal — current value: (.*)$/s;
   134	
   135	async function sendOne(item: ProposedCrmWrite, householdKey: string): Promise<void> {
   136	  // Defense-in-depth: the card is expected to disable Approve while a field
   137	  // item's finalValue is blank, but the store must never fire a network call
   138	  // for one even if that guard is somehow bypassed.
   139	  if (item.kind === 'field' && (item.finalValue ?? '').trim() === '') return;
   140	
   141	  // Set once, on this item's FIRST send attempt, then reused verbatim on
   142	  // every retry (approve() re-fetches the item fresh from the store each
   143	  // time it's called, so a manual Retry sees the value persisted below).
   144	  // Never regenerated per attempt — that would defeat the backend's
   145	  // retry-vs-fresh-approval dedup guarantee (see the field's doc comment).
   146	  const requestedAt = item.requestedAt ?? newRequestedAt();
   147	  setItemClearingError(item.id, { status: 'sending', requestedAt });
   148	  try {
   149	    const receipt =
   150	      item.kind === 'note'
   151	        ? await crmCreateNote({
   152	            matterId: item.matterId,
   153	            title: item.title,
   154	            body: item.body,
   155	            sourceRef: item.sourceRef,
   156	            householdKey,
   157	            requestedAt,
   158	          })
   159	        : item.kind === 'task'
   160	          ? await crmCreateTask({
   161	              matterId: item.matterId,
   162	              title: item.title,
   163	              description: item.body,
   164	              ...(item.dueDate !== undefined ? { dueDate: item.dueDate } : {}),
   165	              sourceRef: item.sourceRef,
   166	              householdKey,
   167	              requestedAt,
   168	            })
   169	          : await crmUpdateField({
   170	              matterId: item.matterId,
   171	              householdKey,
   172	              field: item.field ?? '',
   173	              existingValue: item.existingValue ?? '',
   174	              newValue: item.newValue ?? '',
   175	              finalValue: item.finalValue ?? '',
   176	              sourceRef: item.sourceRef,
   177	              requestedAt,
   178	            });
   179	    setItemClearingError(item.id, { status: 'sent', remoteId: receipt.remoteId });
   180	  } catch (err) {
   181	    const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
   182	    const staleMatch = STALE_FIELD_VALUE_RE.exec(message);
   183	    if (staleMatch) {
   184	      // Coordinator review catch (P2): never blind-overwrite. Re-render the 3
   185	      // columns with the fresh live value instead of the stale one the
   186	      // proposal was drafted against — AND rebuild finalValue from it. The
   187	      // OLD blend was computed against the OLD existingValue and never
   188	      // accounts for the concurrent edit; leaving it in place let a later
   189	      // retry (which resends this refreshed existingValue) pass the
   190	      // backend's stale-guard and silently overwrite the concurrent change
   191	      // the moment the live value stopped drifting further. No provider
   192	      // handle survives a stale rejection (nothing is persisted on the
   193	      // item), so this is always composeFieldBlend's deterministic
   194	      // concatenation, even for an originally AI-blended narrative field —
   195	      // correct-shape and never drops either side's content, which is what
   196	      // matters for a rebuild the advisor hasn't reviewed yet.
   197	      const freshExistingValue = staleMatch[1] ?? '';
   198	      const rebuiltFinalValue = await composeFieldBlend({
   199	        field: item.field ?? '',
   200	        existingValue: freshExistingValue,
   201	        newValue: item.newValue ?? '',
   202	      });
   203	      setItem(item.id, {
   204	        status: 'stale',
   205	        error: message,
   206	        existingValue: freshExistingValue,
   207	        finalValue: rebuiltFinalValue,
   208	      });
   209	      return;
   210	    }
   211	    const status: CrmWriteStatus = message.includes('verification pending') ? 'verify_pending' : 'failed';
   212	    setItem(item.id, { status, error: message });
   213	  }
   214	}
   215	
   216	export const useCrmWriteQueueStore = create<CrmWriteQueueState>((set, get) => ({
   217	  items: [],
   218	
   219	  enqueue: (item) => {
   220	    set((state) => ({
   221	      items: [...state.items, { ...item, id: newId(), status: 'proposed' }],
   222	    }));
   223	  },
   224	
   225	  approve: async (ids, householdKey) => {
   226	    // Sequential: the backend's ~1 rps gate makes parallel sends pointless,
   227	    // and it keeps per-row status updates easy to follow in the UI.
   228	    for (const id of ids) {
   229	      const item = get().items.find((i) => i.id === id);
   230	      if (!item) continue;
   231	      await sendOne(item, householdKey);
   232	    }
   233	  },
   234	
   235	  dismiss: (id) => {
   236	    set((state) => ({ items: state.items.filter((i) => i.id !== id) }));
   237	  },
   238	
   239	  updateFinalValue: (id, finalValue) => {
   240	    setItem(id, { finalValue });
   241	  },
   242	
   243	  enqueueFieldUpdate: async (args) => {
   244	    // Codex review catch (P2): mirrors the backend's validate_field_is_writable
   245	    // — enqueueing a field the desktop app doesn't accept would show the
   246	    // advisor an approval-ready change that's guaranteed to fail every time.
   247	    if (!isWritableField(args.field)) {
   248	      throw new Error(`"${args.field}" is not a writable Wealthbox field yet.`);
   249	    }
   250	    const finalValue = args.provider
   251	      ? await composeFieldBlend({
   252	          field: args.field,
   253	          existingValue: args.existingValue,
   254	          newValue: args.newValue,
   255	          provider: args.provider,
   256	          onBeforeProviderCall: args.onBeforeProviderCall,
   257	        })
   258	      : await composeFieldBlend({
   259	          field: args.field,
   260	          existingValue: args.existingValue,
   261	          newValue: args.newValue,
   262	        });
   263	    get().enqueue({
   264	      kind: 'field',
   265	      matterId: args.matterId,
   266	      title: args.title,
   267	      body: '',
   268	      field: args.field,
   269	      existingValue: args.existingValue,
   270	      newValue: args.newValue,
   271	      finalValue,
   272	      sourceRef: args.sourceRef,
   273	    });
   274	  },
   275	}));
